                                                         華潤萬象生活有限公司 China Resources Mixc Lifestyle Services Limited   005




公司簡介
COMPANY PROFILE


                         華潤萬象生活有限公司（以下簡稱：華潤萬象生活，股票代碼1209.HK）是世界500強企
                         業華潤集團旗下戰略業務單元華潤置地的成員公司，是中國領先的物業管理及商業運營
                         服務商。華潤萬象生活堅持圍繞「成為客戶信賴和喜愛的城市品質生活服務商」的企業
                         願景，憑借卓越的運營服務能力、豐富的生態資源及強大的一體化會員體系，致力打造
                         「全業態、全客戶、全產品與全服務」的城市品質生活服務平台。

                          商管業務聚焦高端與品質購物中心賽道，以「萬象城」、「萬象天地」、「萬象匯」為
                          核心品牌，憑借行業領先的商業運營能力，堅持高品質規模發展，持續打造「中國商業
                          運營管理第一品牌」。物管業務聚焦品質社區、城市公共空間、寫字樓及產業園三大賽
                          道，以「萬象服務」為核心品牌，堅持全域、全業態佈局，致力成為「中國最具影響力
                          的城市空間運營服務商」。

                          截至2021年12月31日，華潤萬象生活提供物業管理服務的住宅及商業物業建築面積已
                          達約1.648億平方米，提供商業運營服務的購物中心及寫字樓建築面積約9.0百萬平方
                          米。

                          未來，公司將加大對科技能力的投入，將科技創新作為公司最核心的優勢，持續打造一
                          體化生態體系，助力城市發展，引領美好生活。



  China Resources Mixc Lifestyle Services Limited (“CR Mixc Lifestyle”) (stock code: 1209.
  HK) is a member of CR Land, a strategic business unit of CR Group, a Fortune Global
  500 company, and is the leading property management and commercial operational
  services provider in China. Upholding to our corporate vision to “become a trustworthy
  and popular urban quality-life service provider among customers”, CR Mixc Lifestyle
  fully capitalizes on the superior operational capabilities, abundant ecological resources
  and strong integrated membership system to build an urban quality-life service platform
  featured with “multiple property types, all customers and full spectrum of product and
  service offerings”.

  Our commercial management business focuses on high-end and high-quality shopping
  mall segment and, by promoting “MIXC”, MIXC World” and “MIXONE” as our core
  brands and leveraging on our industrial-leading commercial operational capabilities,
  our aim of high quality and large- scale development has been persistently pursued,
  striving to become “the PRC’s first brand in commercial operational and management”.
  For property management business, concentrating on three main segments, namely
  high-quality community, urban public space and office buildings and industrial parks,
  and using “MIXC Services” as the core brand, we uphold to our business layout
  of covering all property types across all regions so as to become “the PRC’s most
  influential urban space operational service provider”.

  As of 31 December 2021, the GFA of the residential and commercial properties under
  our property management services was approximately 164.8 million sq.m. and the GFA
  of the shopping malls and office buildings under our commercial operational services
  was approximately 9.0 million sq.m.

  In the future, the Company will further increase investment in technology and take
  technological innovation as the core edge to continuously strengthen our integrated
  ecosystem, facilitate urban development and play a leading role in creating people’s
  happy life.

主席報告
CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT

本人欣然向各位股東提呈本集團二零二一年      I am pleased to present the annual business review and outlook of the
十二月三十一日止之年度業績回顧與展望。      Group for the year ended 31 December 2021 to the shareholders.

二零二一年，世界經濟持續受到新冠疫情衝      During 2021, the global economy continued to be suffered from
擊。面對複雜的外部形勢和變種病毒的考驗，     COVID-19. In face of the complicated external situation and the
中國全年國內生產總值同比增長8.1%，國內社   challenges brought by the variant virus, the GDP of the PRC recorded a
會消費品零售總額突破人民幣40萬億元，成為    YoY growth of 8.1%, while the total domestic retail sales of consumer
全球第二大消費市場，在「十四五」開局邁出高    goods exceeded RMB40 trillion, enabling the PRC to become the
質量發展堅實一步。近年來，國家始終堅持「房    world’s second largest consumer market and demonstrating a solid
住不炒」定位，房地產行業面臨調整變革。一方    step towards quality development of the “14th Five-Year Plan”. In
面，馬太效應愈發顯著、行業整合加速，另一     recent years, under the principle of “houses are for living in, not for
方面，綜合性房企商管及物管業務獨立運營、     speculation”, the PRC real estate industry was subject to adjustment
積極融入資本市場成為新趨勢。商管業務得益     and reformation. On the one hand, with more notable Matthew Effect,
於奢侈品類高端消費回流趨勢延續、新中產人     the industry integration had been accelerating, while on the other
群逐步壯大及Z世代崛起，國內高端及品質賽道    hand, the separate operations of the commercial management and
商業市場快速增長。物管業務持續得到國家政     property management businesses of real estate conglomerates for
策支持利好，迎來重要發展契機；同時，資本     capital market integration had become a new trend. The commercial
市場物管板塊估值顯著分化以及其各自母公司     management business was benefited from the continued returning
債務風險促使物管業務出售，頭部物企迎來整     of high-end consumption of luxury goods, the expanding population
合窗口期。                    of middle class and the growing Z Generation, and the rapid growth
                         of high-end and high quality business market. Supported by the
                         favourable national policies, the property management business
                         enjoyed major development opportunities. Meanwhile, the significant
                         valuation differentiation of the property management business in the
                         capital market and the debt risk of their respective parent company
                         resulted in disposal of the property management business. The leading
                         property management companies entered into the window phase of
                         integration.

                                commercial operational and property management services.

截至二零二一年十二月三十一日止年度內，本            During the year ended 31 December 2021, the Group’s revenue
集團收入為人民幣8,875.4百萬元，較去年同期        amounted to RMB8,875.4 million, representing an increase of 30.9%
增長30.9%，主要由於：(i)住宅物業管理服務的       as compared with the corresponding period of last year. Such increase
在管建築面積增加；(ii)在營商業項目數量上升         was primarily due to: (i) the increase in the GFA under management
及商業項目業績提升，令本集團商業運營服務            of residential property management services; (ii) the increase in our
收入增加；(iii)本集團新增第三方商業項目數量        revenue from commercial operational services due to the rise in the
上升，令商業項目的開業前管理服務收入增加。           number of commercial projects under operation and the improvement
                                in the performance of business projects; (iii) the increase in our newly
                                added third party commercial projects which resulted in the increase
                                in our revenue from the pre-opening management services of the
                                commercial projects.

     外匯風險                                     FOREIGN CURRENCY RISK

     由於本集團業務主要於中國進行，本集團主要                     As the Group’s business is mainly conducted in the PRC, we mainly
     採用人民幣作為結算貨幣。截至二零二一年                      take RMB as the settlement currency. As of 31 December 2021, non-
     十二月三十一日止，非人民幣資產及負債主要                     RMB assets and liabilities mainly included the cash of HK$69.9 million,
     為現金港幣69.9百萬元、12,745.9美元及銀行               US$12,745.9, and the bank borrowings of HK$980.0 million. The
     借款港幣980.0百萬元。管理層認為本集團在經                  management believed that the operation of the Group was not exposed
     營方面並無重大外匯風險，人民幣匯率波動不                     to material foreign currency risk. No significant impact was caused by
     會對本集團財務狀況帶來顯著影響。本集團目                     the fluctuation of RMB exchange rate on the Group’s financial position.
     前並無外幣風險對沖政策，然而，管理層將對                     Meanwhile, the Group currently does not hedge its foreign exchange
     外匯風險敞口實施動態監控並將根據市場環境                     risk but dynamically monitored the foreign exchange exposure and
     的變化進行必要調整。                               made necessary adjustments in accordance with the change in market

作出客觀決策以保障本公司及其股東的利益。                       success of the Company by directing and supervising its affairs. The
董事會授予本集團高級管理層有關本集團日常                       Board makes decisions objectively to safeguard in the interests of the
管理及營運的權限及職責。                               Company and its shareholders. The Board has delegated the authority

     本公司在為董事會提名新董事時遵循公平及透                        The Company follows a fair and transparent procedure for the
     明的程序，實現技能、經驗與成員多元化方面                        appointment of new Directors for the Board to achieve a balance of
     的平衡，以切合本公司戰略目標的要求及具體                        skills and experience and diversity to achieve the Company’s strategic
     業務需求。本公司深知成員多元化可為董事會                        focus and specific business needs. Recognising the vitality of diversity

     風險管理及內部監控                    RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROLS

     董事會負責本公司的風險管理及內部監控系統         The Board is responsible for the Company’s risk management and
     及持續檢討其成效。本集團內部審核團隊及高         internal control systems and for reviewing its effectiveness on an
     級管理層則檢討本集團風險管理及內部監控系         ongoing basis. The Group’s internal audit team and senior management
     統的成效。審核委員會每年至少開會兩次，以         conduct reviews of the effectiveness of the risk management and
     檢討內部審核團隊及高級管理層的調查結果及         internal control systems of the Group. The Audit Committee reviews
     推薦建議，並向董事會匯報有關檢討結果。          the findings and recommendations of the internal audit team and the
                                  senior management in their meetings at least twice a year and reports
                                  to the Board on such review.

     風險管理及內部監控系統的設計旨在管理（而非        The risk management and internal control systems are designed to
     消除）業務風險、防止本集團資產受到欺詐或其        manage, rather than eliminate business risk; to help safeguard the
     他違規情況的損害，以及提供合理（但非絕對）        Group’s assets against fraud and other irregularities; and to give
     保證以防止出現重大財務錯誤陳述或虧損。此         reasonable, but not absolute, assurance against material financial
     外，該系統可為妥善保存公平會計記錄提供基         misstatement or loss. In addition, it should provide a basis for the
     礎，並協助本公司遵守相關規則及法規。           maintenance of proper and fair accounting records and assist in the
                                  compliance with relevant rules and regulations.

     自二零二一年一月一日至本年報刊發日期，董         From 1 January 2021 to the date of this annual report, the Board,
     事會已通過審核委員會檢討本集團風險管理及         through the Audit Committee, reviewed the overall effectiveness of
     內部監控系統的整體有效性，涵蓋財務、營運         the Group’s risk management and internal control systems, covering
     以及合規監控及風險管理職能以及風險評估結         financial, operational and compliance controls and risk management
     果。                           functions and results of risk assessment.

     董事會相信，概無存在可能影響本公司股東的         The Board believes that there are no material internal control
     重大內部監控缺陷，並已落實有效、充分的風         deficiencies that may affect the shareholders of the Company and an
     險管理及內部控制系統以保障本集團的資產。         effective and adequate risk management and internal control system
     審核委員會及高級管理層持續共同監控風險管         is in place to safeguard the assets of the Group. The Audit Committee
     理政策的實施，以確保政策及實施有效及充分。        and senior management will together monitor the implementation of
                                  risk management policies on an ongoing basis to ensure the policies
                                  and implementation are effective and sufficient.

     披露內幕消息                       DISCLOSURE OF INSIDE INFORMATION

     本集團已參考證券及期貨事務監察委員會頒佈         The Group has in place a framework for the disclosure of inside
     的內幕消息披露指引，設置有關披露內幕消息         information by reference to the Guidelines on Disclosure of Inside
     的框架。此框架列出合適及時地處理及發放內         Information issued by the Securities and Futures Commission. The
     幕消息的程序及內部控制，例如下列步驟：確         framework sets out the procedures and internal controls for the
     定充足詳情、對事宜及其對本公司可能產生的         handling and dissemination of inside information in an appropriate
     影響進行內部評估，在需要時尋求專業意見，         and timely manner, such as steps to ascertain sufficient details,
     並核實事實。向公眾全面披露資料前，知悉有         conduct internal assessment of the matter and its likely impact on the
     關消息的任何人士必須確定嚴守保密，不得買         Company, seek professional advice where required and verification
     賣本公司任何證券。                    of the facts. Before the information is fully disclosed to the public,
                                  any persons who possess the knowledge of such information must
                                  ensure strict confidentiality and must not deal in any of the Company’s
                                  securities.

主要業務                     PRINCIPAL ACTIVITIES

我們是中國領先的物業管理及商業運營服務供     The Group is a leading property management and commercial
應商。本集團為最大的住宅物業組合之一提供     operational service provider in China. The Group provides property
物業管理及增值服務，亦管理及運營購物中心     management and value-added services to one of the largest portfolios
和寫字樓等多種不斷擴大的商業物業組合。      of residential properties, and also manages and operates a diversified

     均為華潤置地的全資附屬公司，故根據上市規         Shenzhen Qianhai and CR Shenzhen are wholly-owned subsidiaries

與利益相關方的關係              RELATIONSHIP WITH STAKEHOLDERS

本集團深明僱員、客戶及業務夥伴是其可持續   The Group recognizes that employees, customers and business
發展的關鍵。本集團致力與僱員建立密切及關   partners are keys to its sustainable development. The Group is
懷的關係，為客戶提供優質服務，並加強與其   committed to establishing a close and caring relationship with its
業務夥伴的合作。本公司為員工提供公平及安   employees, providing quality services to its customers and enhancing
全的工作環境，促進員工多元化發展，並基於   cooperation with its business partners. The Company provides a
彼等的優點及表現提供具競爭力的薪酬及福利   fair and safe workplace, promotes diversity to its staff, provides
以及職業發展機會。本集團亦持續為僱員提供   competitive remuneration and benefits and career development
充分的培訓及發展資源，讓彼等了解最新市場   opportunities based on their merits and performance. The Group also
及行業發展資訊，同時提高彼等的表現及在崗   puts ongoing efforts to provide adequate trainings and development
位中實現自我發展。有關本集團僱員的進一步   resources to the employees so that they can keep abreast of the
詳情載於本報告「人力資源」一段。       latest development of the market and the industry and, at the same
                       time, improve their performance and self-fulfillment in their positions.
                       Further details in relation to the Group’s employees are set out in the
                       paragraph headed “Human Resources” in this report.

本集團深明與客戶保持良好關係的重要性，為   The Group understands that it is important to maintain good
客戶提供滿足彼等需要及要求的產品。本集團   relationship with customers and provide the products in a way that
透過與客戶持續互動以洞悉不同產品日益轉變   satisfies needs and requirements of the customers. The Group
的市場需求，使本集團能夠積極回應市場需    enhances the relationship by continuous interaction with customers to
要，藉此增強彼此關係。本集團亦設立程序處   gain insight on the changing market demand for the products so that
理客戶投訴，確保及時迅速解決客戶投訴。    the Group can respond proactively. The Group has also established
                       procedures in place for handling customers’ complaints to ensure
                       customers’ complaints are dealt with in a prompt and timely manner.

本集團亦致力與供應商及承包商等長期業務夥   The Group is also dedicated to developing good relationship with
伴建立良好關係，確保本集團業務穩定發展。   suppliers and contractors as long-term business partners to ensure
本集團持續透過積極有效的方式溝通，加強與   stability of the Group’s business. The Group reinforces business
供應商及承包商的業務夥伴關係。        partnerships with suppliers and contractors by ongoing communication
                       in a proactive manner.

首次公開發售所得款項淨額用途             USE OF NET PROCEEDS FROM INITIAL PUBLIC
                           OFFERING

本集團股份於二零二零年十二月九日於聯交所       The shares of the Group were successfully listed on the Stock
成功上市（「上市」），經扣除承銷費用及相關開     Exchange on 9 December 2020 (the “Listing”), with total net proceeds
支，總上市所得款項淨額約為人民幣11,600.4   of the Listing amounted to approximately RMB11,600.4 million after
百萬元。                       deducting the underwriting fees and relevant expenses.

誠如招股章程所載，本集團擬將該等所得款項       As set out in the Prospectus, the Group intends to use the proceeds
作以下用途：(i)所得款項淨額的約60%用於擴大   for the purposes and in the amounts set forth below: (i) approximately
物業管理和商業運營業務的戰略投資和收購；       60% of the net proceeds for making strategic investments and
(ii)所得款項淨額的約15%用於提供增值服務和   acquisitions to expand our property management and commercial
本行業上下游供應鏈的戰略性投資；(iii)所得款   operational businesses; (ii) approximately 15% of the net proceeds for
項淨額的約15%用於投資信息技術系統和智能      pursuing strategic investment in providers of value-added services and
化社區；(iv)所得款項淨額的約10%用於營運資   across the upstream and downstream supply chain of our industry; (iii)
金及一般公司用途。                  approximately 15% of the net proceeds for investing in information
                           technology systems and smart communities; and (iv) approximately
                           10% of the net proceeds for working capital and general corporate

我們的目標乃對綜合財務報表整體是否不存在     Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the

理保證，並出具包括我們意見的核數師報告。     misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s

外別無其他目的。我們概不就本報告的內容對     a body, and for no other purpose. We do not assume responsibility
任何其他人士負責或承擔責任。           towards or accept liability to any other person for the contents of this
                         report.

錯誤陳述。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯誤引起，     always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements
如果合理預期其單獨或匯總起來可能影響使用     can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if, individually
者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，則有     or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to influence the
關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。            economic decisions of users taken on the basis of these consolidated
                         financial statements.

         港會計師公會」）頒佈的香港財務報告準則         with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”)

         則」）及詮釋）、香港公認會計原則以及香         issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants
         港公司條例的披露規定編製。該等財務報          (“HKICPA”), accounting principles generally accepted in Hong
         表乃按歷史成本法編製，除投資物業和按          Kong and the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong
         公允價值計量且其變動計入當期損益的金          Companies Ordinance. They have been prepared under the

         表乃以人民幣（「人民幣」）呈列，除另有指        financial assets at fair value through profit or loss which have
         明外，所有數值取整至最接近千位數。           been measured at fair value. These financial statements are
                                     presented in Renminbi (“RMB”) and all values are rounded to the

 考概念框架以釐定資產或負債之構成之確          add to HKFRS 3 an exception to its recognition principle for an
 認原則加入一項例外情況。該例外情況規          entity to refer to the Conceptual Framework to determine what
 定，對於屬香港會計準則第37號或香港          constitutes an asset or a liability. The exception specifies that, for

     2.4 關鍵會計政策概要                 2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING
                                      POLICIES

         本集團於編製本報告所載財務報表時採納          The principal accounting policies adopted by the Group in arriving
         的主要會計政策載列如下                 at these financial statements set out in this report are set out
                                     below:

         於合營企業投資                     Investments in joint ventures

         合營企業為一項合營安排，據此擁有安排          A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement whereby the parties
         共同控制權的訂約方有權享有合營安企業          that have joint control of the arrangement have rights to the
         排的資產淨值。共同控制權指按照合同協          net assets of the joint venture. Joint control is the contractually
         定對一項安排所共有的控制權，僅在相關          agreed sharing of control of an arrangement, which exists
         活動必須獲得共同享有控制權的各方一致          only when decisions about the relevant activities require the
         同意方能決定時存在。                  unanimous consent of the parties sharing control.

         本集團於合營企業的投資在綜合財務狀況          The Group’s investments in joint ventures are stated in the
         表中，以按照權益法計算的本集團所佔淨          consolidated statement of financial position at the Group’s share
         資產份額減去任何減值損失後的餘額列           of net assets under the equity method of accounting, less any
         示。會計政策存在不一致的，應當對其進          impairment losses. Adjustments are made to bring into line any
         行調整。                        dissimilar accounting policies that may exist.

         本集團所佔合營企業收購後的業績和其他          The Group’s share of the post-acquisition results and other
         綜合收益的份額分別計入綜合損益表及其          comprehensive income of joint ventures is included in the
         他全面收益表。另外，當合營企業的權益          consolidated statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive
         發生變化，如適用，本集團將在綜合權益          income. In addition, when there has been a change recognised
         變動表中確認變化的相應份額。本集團與          directly in the equity of the joint venture, the Group recognises
         合營企業之間因交易產生的未實現收益或          its share of any changes, when applicable, in the consolidated
         損失，在本集團於合營企業中的投資的範          statement of changes in equity. Unrealised gains and losses
                                     resulting from transactions between the Group and its joint
         圍內予以抵銷，除非有證據表明未實現的
                                     ventures are eliminated to the extent of the Group’s investments
         損失屬於所轉讓資產發生減值損失。本集
                                     in the joint ventures, except where unrealised losses provide
         團對合營企業的投資包含因收購合營企業
                                     evidence of an impairment of the assets transferred. Goodwill
         所產生的商譽。
                                     arising from the acquisition of joint ventures is included as part of
                                     the Group’s investments in joint ventures.

         若對聯營公司的投資轉變為對合營企業的          If an investment in an associate becomes an investment in a joint
         投資或者反之，剩餘權益不再重新計量。          venture or vice versa, the retained interest is not remeasured.
         相反，該投資將繼續按權益法進行核算。          Instead, the investment continues to be accounted for under
         在所有其他情況下，一旦失去對聯營公司          the equity method. In all other cases, upon loss of significant
         的重大影響或對合營企業的共同控制，本          influence over the associate or joint control over the joint
         集團按照公允價值計量或確認任何剩餘投          venture, the Group measures and recognises any retained
         資。當失去重大影響或共同控制時，聯營          investment at its fair value. Any difference between the carrying
         公司或合營企業的賬面價值與剩餘投資的          amount of the associate or joint venture upon loss of significant
         公允價值之差以及處置收益被確認為損           influence or joint control and the fair value of the retained

  公允價值計量                  Fair value measurement

  本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計量其投      The Group measures its investment properties and financial
  資物業及以公允價值計量且其變動計入當      assets at fair value through profit or loss at fair value at the end
  期收益的金融資產。公允價值指於計量日      of each reporting period. Fair value is the price that would be
  期市場參與者之間的有序交易中，就出售      received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly
  資產所收取的價格或轉讓負債所支付的價      transaction between market participants at the measurement
  格。公允價值計量乃基於假設出售資產或      date. The fair value measurement is based on the presumption
  轉讓負債的交易於資產或負債的主要市       that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability takes
  場，或在未有主要市場的情況下，則於資      place either in the principal market for the asset or liability, or
  產或負債最有利市場進行。主要或最有利      in the absence of a principal market, in the most advantageous
  市場須位於本集團能到達的地方。資產或      market for the asset or liability. The principal or the most
  負債的公允價值乃使用市場參與者為資產      advantageous market must be accessible by the Group. The fair
  或負債定價所用的假設計量（假設市場參      value of an asset or a liability is measured using the assumptions
  與者依照彼等最佳經濟利益行事）。        that market participants would use when pricing the asset or
                          liability, assuming that market participants act in their economic
                          best interest.

  非金融資產公允價值之計量則參考市場參      A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into
  與者可從使用該資產得到之最高及最佳效      account a market participant’s ability to generate economic
  用，或把該資產售予另一可從使用該資產      benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by
  得到最高及最佳效用之市場參與者所產生      selling it to another market participant that would use the asset in
  之經濟效益。                  its highest and best use.

2.4 關鍵會計政策概要（續）          2.4 SUMMARY OF SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING
                             POLICIES (continued)
  金融資產減值                    Impairment of financial assets

  本集團就所有並非按公允價值計量且其變        The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses
  動計入當期損益而持有的債務工具確認預        (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value through
  期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。預       profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference between the
  期信貸虧損基於按照合同到期的合同現金        contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and
  流量與本集團預計收到的所有現金流量之        all the cash flows that the Group expects to receive, discounted
  間的差額，按接近原始實際利率的利率貼        at an approximation of the original effective interest rate. The
  現。預期現金流量將包括出售所持抵押品        expected cash flows will include cash flows from the sale of
  或為合同條款一部分的其他信用增強措施        collateral held or other credit enhancements that are integral to
  的現金流量。                    the contractual terms.

  一般法                       General approach

  預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。就自初始        ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for
  確認起信貸風險並未大幅增加的信貸風險        which there has not been a significant increase in credit risk since
  而言，預期信貸虧損就可能於未來12個        initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses that result
  月內發生的違約事件導致的信貸虧損作出        from default events that are possible within the next 12 months
  撥備（12個月預期信貸虧損）。就自初始       (a 12-month ECL). For those credit exposures for which there has
  確認起信貸風險已大幅增加的信貸風險而        been a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,
  言，須於風險的餘下年期內就信貸虧損作        a loss allowance is required for credit losses expected over the
  出虧損撥備（不論違約時間）（ 整個存續期      remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the
  預期信貸虧損）。                  default (a lifetime ECL).

  於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具的信        At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the
  貸風險自初始確認起是否已大幅增加。在        credit risk on a financial instrument has increased significantly
  作出評估時，本集團將金融工具於報告日        since initial recognition. When making the assessment,
  期發生違約的風險與金融工具於初始確認        the Group compares the risk of a default occurring on the
  日期發生違約的風險進行比較，考慮無需        financial instrument as at the reporting date with the risk of a
  過多成本或努力即可獲得的合理有支持資        default occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of
  料（包括歷史及前瞻性資料）。            initial recognition and considers reasonable and supportable
                            information that is available without undue cost or effort,
                            including historical and forward-looking information.

  當合同付款逾期90天時，本集團認為金        The Group considers a financial asset in default when contractual
  融資產已違約。然而，在若干情況下，當        payments are 90 days past due. However, in certain cases,
  內部或外部資料表明本集團不大可能收回        the Group may also consider a financial asset to be in default
  全部未收回的合同款項（在考慮本集團持        when internal or external information indicates that the Group
  有的任何信貸增強措施前）時，本集團亦        is unlikely to receive the outstanding contractual amounts in
  可能將金融資產視為違約。當合理預期無        full before taking into account any credit enhancements held
  法收回合同現金流量時，金融資產予以撇        by the Group. A financial asset is written off when there is no
  銷。                        reasonable expectation of recovering the contractual cash flows.

         如補助涉及資產，則公允價值計入遞延收          Where the grant relates to an asset, the fair value is credited to
         入賬戶，並按等額年度分期在相關資產的          a deferred income account and is released to the consolidated
         預計可使用年期撥回綜合損益及其他全面          statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income over
         收益表，或透過降低折舊費用而從資產的          the expected useful life of the relevant asset by equal annual
         賬面值中扣除並撥回綜合損益及其他全面          instalments or deducted from the carrying amount of the asset
         收益表。                        and released to the consolidated statement of profit or loss and
                                     other comprehensive income by way of a reduced depreciation

         認。                          the consideration to which the Group expects to be entitled in

3.   主要會計判斷及估計            3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS
                               AND ESTIMATES

     編製本集團的財務報表需要管理層作出影        The preparation of the Group’s financial statements requires
     響收入、開支、資產及負債的呈報金額、        management to make judgements, estimates and assumptions
     其相應披露及或然負債披露的判斷、估計        that affect the reported amounts of revenues, expenses, assets
     及假設。該等假設及估計的不確定性可能        and liabilities, and their accompanying disclosures, and the
     導致未來需要對受影響的資產或負債的賬        disclosure of contingent liabilities. Uncertainty about these
     面值作出重大調整。                 assumptions and estimates could result in outcomes that could
                               require a material adjustment to the carrying amounts of the
                               assets or liabilities affected in the future.

     判斷                        Judgements

     在應用本集團的會計政策過程中，除涉及        In the process of applying the Group’s accounting policies,
     對財務報表中已確認金額構成最大影響的        management has made the following judgements, apart from

     本集團於各報告期末評估所有非金融資產         The Group assesses whether there are any indicators of
     是否存在任何減值跡象。在有跡象表明賬         impairment for all non-financial assets at the end of each
     面值可能無法收回時，對非金融資產進行         reporting period. Non-financial assets are tested for impairment
     減值測試。當資產或現金產生單位的賬面         when there are indicators that the carrying amounts may not
     值超過其可收回金額（即公允價值減出售         be recoverable. An impairment exists when the carrying value
     成本與使用價值兩者中的較高者）時，即         of an asset or a cash-generating unit exceeds its recoverable
     存在減值。公允價值減出售成本的計算基         amount, which is the higher of its fair value less costs of
     於類似資產的公平交易中具有約束力的交         disposal and its value in use. The calculation of the fair value less
     易的現有數據，或可觀察市場價格減去          costs of disposal is based on available data from binding sales
     出售資產的增量成本。進行使用價值計算         transactions in an arm’s length transaction of similar assets or
     時，管理層必須估計資產或現金產生單位         observable market prices less incremental costs for disposing
     的預計未來現金流量，並選擇適當的折現         of the asset. When value-in-use calculations are undertaken,
     率，以計算該等現金流量的現值。            management must estimate the expected future cash flows from
                                the asset or cash-generating unit and choose a suitable discount

     租賃—估計增量借款利率                Leases – Estimating the IBR

     本集團無法輕易釐定租賃中隱含的利率，         The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate implicit in a
     因此，本集團使用增量借款利率計量租賃         lease, and therefore, it uses an IBR to measure lease liabilities.
     負債。增量借款利率指本集團在類似經濟         The IBR is the rate of interest that the Group would have to pay to
     環境下按類似期限、以類似抵押品借入取         borrow over a similar term, and with a similar security, the funds
     得價值與使用權資產接近的資產所需的資         necessary to obtain an asset of a similar value to the right-of-
     金而須支付的利率。因此，增量借款利率         use asset in a similar economic environment. The IBR therefore
     反映本集團「須支付」的利率，當並無可         reflects what the Group “would have to pay”, which requires
     觀察利率時（如對並無訂立融資交易的附         estimation when no observable rates are available (such as for
     屬公司而言），或需要調整利率以反映租         subsidiaries that do not enter into financing transactions) or when
     賃條款及條件時（例如，當租賃並非以附         it needs to be adjusted to reflect the terms and conditions of
     屬公司的功能貨幣訂立時），需要估計該         the lease. (for example, when leases are not in the subsidiary’s
     利率。當可觀察輸入數據（如市場利率）可        functional currency). The Group estimates the IBR using
     用時，本集團使用可觀察輸入數據估算遞         observable inputs (such as market interest rates) when available
     增借貸利率並須作出若干實體特定的估計         and is required to make certain entity-specific estimates (such as
     （如附屬公司單獨的信貸評級）。            the subsidiary’s stand-alone credit rating).

         附註：                               Notes:

         (a)   本集團的估值程序                    (a)   Valuation processes of the Group

               本集團投資物業位於中國內地。本集團                 The Group’s investment properties are situated in Mainland China.
               按公允價值計量其投資物業。本集團投                 The Group measures its investment properties at fair value. The
               資物業於二零二一年十二月三十一日及                 fair values of the Group’s investment properties as at 31 December
               二零二零年十二月三十一日的公允價值                 2021 and 31 December 2020 have been determined on the basis

  (b)   估值技術                           (b)   Valuation techniques

        估值中採用的估值方法為收入法。                      The valuation methodology adopted in valuation is the income
                                             approach.

        收入法的運作考慮來自現有租賃的物業                    The income approach operates by takes into account the rental
        租賃收入（就潛在的租賃復歸收入作出適                   income of the properties derived from the existing tenancy
        當撥備），然後以適當的資本化率資本化                   with due allowance for the reversionary income potential of the
        為價值。                                 tenancy, which is then capitalised into the value at an appropriate
                                             capitalisation rate.

        在估計物業公允價值時，物業的最高及                    In estimating the fair value of the properties, the highest and best
        最佳用途為現有用途。                           use of the properties is their current use.

17. 定期存款                 17. TIME DEPOSITS

   餘額指從中國內地信譽卓著的持牌銀行購       The balance represents deposit certificates purchased from a
   買的大額存單，固定年利率為3.5%，原      creditworthy licensed bank in Mainland China earning interest
   訂期限為36個月。定期存款於持有超過三      at a fixed rate of 3.5% per annum with original maturity period
   個月時可予以贖回。定期存款的合同條款       of 36 months. The deposits certificates are redeemable upon
   於特定日期產生的現金流量僅為對本金及       holding for longer than three months. The contractual terms of
   未償還本金利息的付款，並按目標為持有       the time deposits give rise on specified dates to cash flows that
   及獲取合同現金流量的業務模式持有。因       are SPPI on the principal amount outstanding and are held within
   此，定期存款入賬列作按攤銷成本計量的       a business model with the objective to hold in order to collect
   金融資產。                    contractual cash flows. For such purpose, the time deposits are
                            accounted for as financial assets at amortised cost.

     30. 儲備                       30. RESERVES

         本集團於本年度及先前年度的儲備款額及          The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements therein
         其變動於財務報表的綜合權益變動表呈           for the current and prior years are presented in the consolidated
         列。                          statement of changes in equity of the financial statements.

         (A) 合併儲備                    (a) Merger reserve

              本集團的合併儲備指本集團現時旗             The merger reserve of the Group represents the
              下的附屬公司繳足股本總額與本集             difference between the aggregate of the paid-up share
              團就共同控制下的業務合併支付的             capital of the subsidiaries now comprising the Group
              代價之間的差額。                    and the consideration paid by the Group for the business
                                          combination under common control.

         (B) 法定盈餘儲備                  (b) Statutory surplus reserve

              根據中國公司法及集團公司各自之             In accordance with the Company Law of the PRC and the
              組織章程，於中國註冊的各公司須             respective articles of association of the group companies,
              劃撥10%根據中國公認會計準則釐            each of the companies that is domiciled in the PRC
              定的除稅後溢利至法定盈餘儲備，             is required to allocate 10% of its profit after tax, as
              直至達到註冊資本的50%為止。轉            determined in accordance with PRC GAAP, to the statutory
              撥至該儲備須於向股東作出股息分             surplus reserve until the reserve reaches 50% of the
              派前作出。                       registered capital. The transfer to this reserve must be
                                          made before the distribution of a dividend to shareholders.

              法定盈餘儲備為不可分配，除清盤             The statutory surplus reserve is non-distributable except
              情況受有關中國法例所載若干限制             that in the event of liquidation where it can be used to
              的規限，可用作抵銷累計虧損或資             offset accumulated losses or be capitalised as paid-up
              本化為繳足股本外。                   capital, subject to certain restrictions set out in the relevant
                                          PRC regulations.

     36. 金融風險管理目標及政策                         36. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES
                                                 AND POLICIES

         本集團的主要金融工具主要包括與本集團                     The Group’s principal financial instruments mainly include
         業務直接相關的受限制銀行存款、現金及                     restricted bank deposits, cash and cash equivalents, trade and
         現金等價物、貿易及其他應收款項、貿易                     other receivables, trade and other payables, which arise directly
         及其他應付款項。本集團亦擁有其他金融                     from its operations. The Group has other financial assets and
         資產及負債，如租賃負債、應付關聯方款                     liabilities such as lease liabilities, amounts due to related parties
         項及應收關聯方款項。該等金融工具的主                     and amounts due from related parties. The main purpose of
         要目的是為本集團的業務籌集資金。                       these financial instruments is to raise finance for the Group’s
                                                operations.

         本集團金融工具引致的主要風險為利率風                     The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments are
         險、外幣風險、信貸風險及流動資金風                      interest rate risk, foreign currency risk, credit risk and liquidity
         險。通常，本集團於風險管理中採用保守                     risk. Generally, the Group introduces conservative strategies on
         策略。為將本集團的該等風險降至最低，                     its risk management. To keep the Group’s exposure to these
         本集團並無為對沖目的而使用任何衍生工                     risks to a minimum, the Group has not used any derivatives and
         具及其他工具。本集團並無為交易目的持                     other instruments for hedging purposes. The Group does not hold
         有或發行衍生金融工具。董事會審閱及同                     or issue derivative financial instruments for trading purposes. The
         意管理各項該等風險的政策，相關政策概                     board of directors reviews and agrees policies for managing each
         述如下：                                   of these risks and they are summarised below:

         利率風險                                   Interest rate risk

         本集團面臨的利率風險主要與本集團的借                     The Group’s exposure to interest rate risk relates primarily to the
         款有關。本集團借款之利率及還款條款於                     Group’s borrowings. The interest rates and terms of repayment
         財務報表附註28披露。本集團的政策乃為                    of the Group’s borrowings are disclosed in note 28 to the
         其借款取得最優惠的利率。                           financial statements. The Group’s policy is to obtain the most
                                                favourable interest rates available for its borrowings.

         本集團持續評估其面臨的利率風險，以釐                     The Group constantly assesses the interest rate risk it
         定是否需要對沖可能出現的潛在利率風                      encounters to decide whether it is required to hedge against the
         險。                                     possible interest rate risk that may arise.

     36. 金融風險管理目標及政策（續）           36. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT OBJECTIVES
                                      AND POLICIES (continued)
         流動資金風險                      Liquidity risk

         本集團內各經營實體負責自身現金管理，          Individual operating entities within the Group are responsible
         包括現金盈餘的短期投資及籌措貸款以應          for their own cash management, including the short-term
         付預期現金需求；惟如借款超出預定授權          investment of cash surpluses and the raising of loans to cover
         金額，則須獲得管理層及董事批准後，方          expected cash demands, subject to approval by management and
         可進行。                        directors when the borrowings exceed certain predetermined
                                     levels of authority.

         本集團的政策是定期監察流動資金需要及          The Group’s policy is to regularly monitor its liquidity
         其是否已遵守借貸契諾的規定，確保其維          requirements and its compliance with lending covenants to
         持充裕的現金儲備及從主要金融機構取得          ensure that it maintains sufficient reserves of cash and adequate
         足夠的承諾信貸融資，以應付其短期及較          committed lines of funding from major financial institutions to
         長期間的流動資金需求。                 meet its liquidity requirements in the short and longer terms.

         的餘下合同到期時間，基於合同未貼現現          at the end of each year of the Group’s non-derivative financial

         率）基於報告期末的現行利率計算的利息          (including interest payments computed using contractual rates

                                                AND POLICIES (continued)
         資本管理                                         Capital management

         本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團內實體                           The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in
         有能力進行持續經營，並透過優化債務與                           the Group will be able to continue as a going concern while
         權益平衡為股東帶來最大回報。                               maximising the return to shareholders through the optimisation
                                                      of the debt and equity balance.

         本公司董事持續檢討負債與資產比率（為                           The directors of the Company review the liability-to-asset ratio,
         總負債除以總資產），計及資本成本及與                           which is total liabilities divided by total assets, on a continuous
         各類資本相關的風險。本集團將透過籌集                           basis, taking into account the cost of capital and the risks
         新債務及贖回現有債務而平衡整體資本架                           associated with each class of capital. The Group will balance its
         構，並管理負債與資產比率。本集團的整                           overall capital structure through the raising of new debts as well
         體策略於年內維持不變。                                  as the redemption of the existing debts, and manage the liability
                                                      to asset ratio. The Group’s overall strategy remained unchanged
                                                      during the year.
